Generative Models & Diffusion

Learning to create — diffusion, flow matching, and friends that turn noise into images, molecules, and video.

← all themes

ICLR 2026

Adapting Self-Supervised Representations as a Latent Space for Efficient Generation

Problem. Generative models typically require large, spatially redundant latent spaces, increasing training costs and architectural complexity.
Approach. We introduce Representation Tokenizer (RepTok) which represents images using a single continuous latent token from pre-trained SSL encoders, fine-tuning only the token embedding with flow-matching and cosine-similarity regularization.
Contribution. RepTok achieves competitive results on class-conditional ImageNet generation and zero-shot text-to-image synthesis on MS-COCO with extremely limited training budgets.
self-supervised learningflow matchingsingle-token representationcosine-similarity regularization
ICLR 2026

MoGA: Mixture-of-Groups Attention for End-to-End Long Video Generation

Problem. Long video generation with Diffusion Transformers is bottlenecked by quadratic attention scaling, as attention is highly redundant and outputs are dominated by small query-key subsets.
Approach. We introduce Mixture-of-Groups Attention (MoGA), using a lightweight learnable token router to precisely match tokens with semantic-aware routing, integrating seamlessly with FlashAttention and sequence parallelism.
Contribution. An efficient long video generation model produces minute-level, multi-shot 480p videos at 24 fps with a context length of approximately 580k tokens.
sparse attentiontoken routingmixture of expertslong video generation
ICLR 2026

MambaVoiceCloning: Efficient and Expressive Text-to-Speech via State-Space Modeling and Diffusion Control

Problem. Text-to-speech systems struggle to efficiently condition diffusion models while removing computational bottlenecks from attention and recurrence modules, limiting deployment scalability.
Approach. MambaVoiceCloning replaces attention-based conditioning with a fully state-space model (Mamba) architecture across text encoding, temporal modeling, and expressive prosody control, maintaining linear-time complexity with bounded memory.
Contribution. Achieves statistically reliable quality gains over StyleTTS2 and VITS while reducing encoder parameters to 21M and improving throughput by 1.6x.
state-space modelsdiffusion-based ttsmamba encoderadaln modulation
ICLR 2026

Diffusion Transformers with Representation Autoencoders

Problem. Diffusion Transformers remain limited by legacy VAE encoders that introduce large convolutional backbones, weak semantic representations, and low-dimensional latent spaces.
Approach. Replaces VAE autoencoders with Representation Autoencoders combining pretrained semantic encoders (DINO, SigLIP, MAE) and trained decoders, then proposes theoretically motivated solutions to enable DiTs to operate in high-dimensional spaces.
Contribution. Achieves state-of-the-art image generation with FID 1.18 at 256 resolution and 1.13 at 512 on ImageNet.
representation autoencodersdiffusion transformerssemantic encodershigh-dimensional latent spaces
ICLR 2026

Model Already Knows the Best Noise: Bayesian Active Noise Selection via Attention in Video Diffusion Model

Problem. Initial noise choice strongly affects video diffusion quality and prompt alignment, but models lack inherent mechanisms to select optimal seeds.
Approach. ANSE uses Bayesian Active Noise Selection via Attention (BANSA), measuring entropy disagreement across stochastic attention samples to estimate model confidence.
Contribution. Improves video quality and temporal coherence with marginal inference overhead across diverse text-to-video backbones.
active noise selectionbayesian attention samplingentropy disagreementinference-time optimization
ICLR 2026

Dual-Solver: A Generalized ODE Solver for Diffusion Models with Dual Prediction

Problem. Diffusion model sampling requires many model evaluations at inference time, and classical ODE multistep methods have inconsistent design choices for prediction types and integration domains.
Approach. Dual-Solver generalizes multistep samplers with learnable parameters that interpolate among prediction types, select integration domains, and adjust residuals, trained via frozen classifier feedback.
Contribution. Improves FID and CLIP scores in low-NFE regimes across class-conditional and text-to-image generation tasks.
ode multistep methodslearnable parametersprediction type interpolationclassifier-guided training
ICLR 2026

Bridging Piano Transcription and Rendering via Disentangled Score Content and Style

Problem. Expressive performance rendering and automatic piano transcription are inverse tasks but have been addressed independently, missing opportunities for joint modeling.
Approach. Proposes unified framework jointly modeling both tasks by disentangling note-level score content and global performance style, with diffusion-based style recommendation module.
Contribution. Achieves competitive performance on both EPR and APT while enabling effective content-style disentanglement, style transfer, and stylistically appropriate rendering.
sequence-to-sequence transformerscontent-style disentanglementdiffusion-based stylejoint modeling
ICLR 2026

Projected Coupled Diffusion for Test-Time Constrained Joint Generation

Problem. Generating jointly correlated samples from multiple diffusion models while enforcing task-specific constraints without retraining is difficult.
Approach. Projected Coupled Diffusion introduces coupled guidance to coordinate diffusion models and adds projection steps to enforce hard constraints at each denoising step.
Contribution. PCD enables constrained joint generation for image-pair synthesis, object manipulation, and multi-robot motion planning with guaranteed constraint satisfaction.
diffusion couplingconstraint projectionguided sampling
ICLR 2026

Generalised Flow Maps for Few-Step Generative Modelling on Riemannian Manifolds

Problem. Geometric generative models on Riemannian manifolds remain computationally expensive at inference despite applications in protein and chemistry.
Approach. Generalised Flow Maps extend flow map framework to Riemannian manifolds; instantiate with three self-distillation methods for few-step generation.
Contribution. GFMs achieve state-of-the-art sample quality and log-likelihood on geospatial, RNA, and hyperbolic data with single/few-step evaluation.
flow matchingriemannian manifoldsself-distillation
ICLR 2026

Latent Denoising Makes Good Tokenizers

Problem. It remains unclear what properties make tokenizers more effective for generative modeling, and current tokenizers may not align well with downstream denoising objectives used by modern generative models.
Approach. Latent Denoising Tokenizer (LDT) aligns tokenizer embeddings directly with the denoising objective by training tokenizers to reconstruct clean images from latent embeddings corrupted via interpolative noise or random masking.
Contribution. LDT consistently improves generation quality across six representative generative models on class-conditioned ImageNet and text-conditioned MSCOCO benchmarks compared to prior tokenizers.
denoisingtokenizationlatent embedding
ICLR 2026

A Unification of Discrete, Gaussian, and Simplicial Diffusion

Problem. Modeling discrete sequences like DNA, proteins, and language requires choosing between three disparate diffusion methods with different algorithms, theoretical structures, and tradeoffs.
Approach. A unified theory expresses discrete, Gaussian, and simplicial diffusion as different parameterizations of the Wright-Fisher population genetics model, with simplicial and Gaussian diffusion emerging as large-population limits.
Contribution. The theory formally connects likelihoods and hyperparameters; enables training a single model that performs diffusion in any of the three domains at test time while providing stable simplicial diffusion.
wright-fisher modeldiffusion unificationpopulation genetics
ICLR 2026

Eliminating VAE for Fast and High-Resolution Generative Detail Restoration

Problem. Diffusion-based super-resolution is slow at inference and has high memory demands; existing distillation approaches require tile-by-tile processing of high-resolution images due to memory boundaries.
Approach. GenDR-Pix eliminates the VAE bottleneck by using pixel-shuffle operations and multi-stage adversarial distillation with generative feature guidance and random padding augmentation.
Contribution. GenDR-Pix achieves 2.8× acceleration and 60% memory-saving compared to GenDR with negligible visual degradation and can restore 4K images.
distillationadversarial trainingsuper-resolution
ICLR 2026

pi-Flow: Policy-Based Few-Step Generation via Imitation Distillation

Problem. Few-step diffusion distillation typically suffers from format mismatch and quality-diversity tradeoffs due to teachers predicting velocity while students predict shortcuts.
Approach. π-Flow modifies the student flow model output layer to predict a network-free policy at one timestep, which then generates dynamic velocities at future substeps via ODE integration, matched to the teacher through imitation distillation.
Contribution. On ImageNet 256×256, π-Flow achieves 1-NFE FID of 2.85 outperforming previous 1-NFE models; on FLUX.1-12B and Qwen-Image-20B at 4 NFEs achieves substantially better diversity than state-of-the-art methods.
flow matchingdistillationpolicy-based generation
ICLR 2026

Routing Matters in MoE: Scaling Diffusion Transformers with Explicit Routing Guidance

Problem. Mixture-of-Experts applied to Diffusion Transformers yields limited gains because visual tokens exhibit spatial redundancy and functional heterogeneity unlike semantic-dense language tokens, hindering expert specialization.
Approach. ProMoE features a two-step router with explicit routing guidance: conditional routing partitions tokens into conditional/unconditional sets, and prototypical routing refines assignments via learnable prototypes based on semantic content with a routing contrastive loss.
Contribution. ProMoE surpasses state-of-the-art methods on ImageNet benchmark, demonstrating improved expert specialization for vision MoE.
mixture of expertsrouting guidancecontrastive learning
ICLR 2026

SynCoGen: Synthesizable 3D Molecule Generation via Joint Reaction and Coordinate Modeling

Problem. Ensuring synthesizability in generative small molecule design remains a major challenge, and existing efforts are largely confined to 2D graphs, limiting geometry-based conditional generation.
Approach. SynCoGen combines masked graph diffusion and flow matching for synthesizable 3D molecule generation, sampling from the joint distribution of building blocks, chemical reactions, and atomic coordinates using curated SYNSPACE dataset.
Contribution. SynCoGen achieves state-of-the-art performance in unconditional molecule generation and competitive performance in zero-shot molecular linker design and pharmacophore conditioning.
graph diffusionflow matchingmolecular generation
ICLR 2026

SFBD-OMNI: Bridge models for lossy measurement restoration with limited clean samples

Problem. Recovering distributions from abundant noisy samples with limited clean samples is challenging when the corruption process introduces per-sample information loss.
Approach. SFBD-OMNI formulates distribution restoration as a one-sided entropic optimal transport problem and extends Stochastic Forward-Backward Deconvolution to arbitrary measurement models, providing conditions for recovery.
Contribution. SFBD-OMNI substantially improves recovery in the challenging regime of non-identifiable corruption processes, validated through synthetic and real-world experiments.
optimal transportdeconvolutiondistribution restoration
ICLR 2026

ToonComposer: Streamlining Cartoon Production with Generative Post-Keyframing

Problem. Traditional cartoon and anime production requires intensive manual effort across keyframing, inbetweening, and colorization stages, with existing methods handling these separately causing error accumulation.
Approach. ToonComposer unifies inbetweening and colorization into a single post-keyframing stage using sparse sketch injection and a novel spatial low-rank adapter (SLRA) to adapt a video foundation model to cartoon domain.
Contribution. Requiring as few as a single sketch and colored reference, ToonComposer outperforms existing methods in visual quality, motion consistency, and production efficiency.
video generationlow-rank adaptationsketch-guided synthesis
ICLR 2026

CMT: Mid-Training for Efficient Learning of Consistency, Mean Flow, and Flow-Map Models

Problem. Flow map models remain unstable and expensive to train, sensitive to hyperparameters even when initialized from pre-trained diffusion models.
Approach. Introduces mid-training, a lightweight intermediate stage between diffusion pre-training and flow map post-training that learns trajectory-consistent initialization for vision generation.
Contribution. Achieves state-of-the-art two-step FIDs (1.97 on CIFAR-10, 1.32 on ImageNet 64×64, 1.84 on ImageNet 512×512) using up to 98% less training data and GPU time than prior methods.
flow matchingconsistency modelsdiffusion modelstrajectory-consistent initialization
ICLR 2026

FlashWorld: High-quality 3D Scene Generation within Seconds

Problem. Multi-view 3D generation suffers from poor visual quality while 3D-consistent approaches sacrifice realism, with conventional multi-view paradigm unable to balance these trade-offs efficiently.
Approach. Proposes FlashWorld with dual-mode pre-training from video diffusion priors followed by cross-mode post-training distillation that bridges multi-view and 3D-oriented generation while reducing denoising steps.
Contribution. Generates high-quality 3D scenes from single image or text in seconds, 10-100x faster than prior works while maintaining superior rendering quality and 3D consistency.
3d gaussian splattingmulti-view diffusiondistillationvideo priors
ICLR 2026

ProReGen: Progressive Residual Generation under Attribute Correlations

Problem. Attribute correlations in training data prevent deep generative models from synthesizing under-represented attribute combinations, limiting diversity in minority samples.
Approach. Proposes ProReGen using progressive residual generation inspired by Robinson's transformation to orthogonalize inputs, first learning majority samples then expanding with residual layers for minorities.
Contribution. Demonstrates improved synthesis of minority attribute combinations across three benchmark datasets with controlled attribute correlation strengths.
residual generationorthogonalizationcausal mechanismsdata imbalance
ICLR 2026

HoloPart: Generative 3D Part Amodal Segmentation

Problem. 3D part segmentation only identifies visible surfaces, limiting utility for 3D content creation; extending to complete occluded parts while maintaining global consistency is challenging.
Approach. Proposes HoloPart, a diffusion-based model with local attention for fine geometry and global shape context attention, completing incomplete part segments into full 3D parts.
Contribution. Significantly outperforms shape completion methods on new ABO and PartObjaverse-Tiny benchmarks, enabling 3D part amodal segmentation for geometry editing and animation.
diffusion models3d segmentationshape completionamodal completion
ICLR 2026

Preserve and Personalize: Personalized Text-to-Image Diffusion Models without Distributional Drift

Problem. Personalizing text-to-image diffusion models leads to overfitting and distributional drift, where models ignore prompts and replicate reference images rather than balancing subject fidelity with text alignment.
Approach. Introduces Lipschitz-based regularization during personalization to constrain parameter updates and preserve the pretrained distribution, promoting consistency while enabling accurate concept adaptation.
Contribution. Achieves superior performance in visual fidelity and prompt adherence through bounded deviation from original distribution, with computational efficiency over resource-intensive sampling.
lipschitz regularizationpersonalizationdistribution preservation
ICLR 2026

BAR: Refactor the Basis of Autoregressive Visual Generation

Problem. Autoregressive image generation predicts tokens in fixed raster-scan order, relying on human-designed prediction units and orders that limit performance.
Approach. BAR conceptualizes tokens as basis vectors and employs end-to-end learnable transformation matrix to refactor token sequences, discovering effective generation strategies beyond hand-crafted assumptions.
Contribution. Achieves state-of-the-art FID of 1.15 on ImageNet-256, overcoming human biases and advancing image generation including text-to-image synthesis.
basis autoregressive modelslearnable token transformationneural generation
ICLR 2026

Large Scale Diffusion Distillation via Score-Regularized Continuous-Time Consistency

Problem. Scaling continuous-time consistency models to large-scale text-to-image and video diffusion faces infrastructure challenges in Jacobian computation and quality limitations in fine-detail generation.
Approach. Develops FlashAttention-2 JVP kernel for parallel training, proposes score-regularized consistency model incorporating score distillation as regularizer to complement forward-divergence objective.
Contribution. Scales to 14B models and 5-second videos, matches state-of-the-art DMD2 on quality while mitigating mode collapse and improving diversity without GAN tuning.
continuous-time consistencyscore regularizationlarge-scale distillation
ICLR 2026

Target-Aware Video Diffusion Models

Problem. Video generation models lack awareness of which objects actors should interact with, limiting their ability to perform directed human-object interactions.
Approach. Introduce target awareness by encoding target mask spatial information via a special token in text prompts, then fine-tune with cross-attention loss aligning attention maps to the target mask.
Contribution. A target-aware video diffusion model that accurately generates videos of actors performing directed actions on specified objects and enables zero-shot 3D HOI motion synthesis.
cross-attention losstarget encodingvideo diffusion
ICLR 2026

PiCa: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning with Column Space Projection

Problem. Fine-tuning large foundation models is computationally prohibitive, and parameter-efficient methods lack solid theoretical foundations.
Approach. Project gradients onto the principal column space of pre-trained weights using Kronecker-Factored Approximate Curvature with a novel weight-sharing strategy.
Contribution. PiCa consistently outperforms state-of-the-art PEFT baselines across NLP and vision tasks under comparable or smaller parameter budgets with theoretical rigor.
gradient projectioncolumn space projectionweight sharing
ICLR 2026

When Scores Learn Geometry: Rate Separations under the Manifold Hypothesis

Problem. Score-based methods are interpreted as learning distributions but this perspective doesn't fully explain their empirical success.
Approach. Analyze scores in small-σ regime revealing a sharp separation of scales where manifold information is Θ(σ⁻²) stronger than distributional information.
Contribution. Shows geometric learning (data manifold) is O(σ⁻²) easier than distributional learning, providing theoretical justification for score-based methods' effectiveness.
score-based learningmanifold hypothesisasymptotic analysis
ICLR 2026

Diffusion & Adversarial Schrödinger Bridges via Iterative Proportional Markovian Fitting

Problem. Iterative Markovian Fitting for Schrödinger Bridge problems requires heuristic modification for practical stability, lacking unified theoretical framework.
Approach. Demonstrate connection between modified IMF and Iterative Proportional Fitting, unifying both as Iterative Proportional Markovian Fitting procedure.
Contribution. IPMF procedure enables flexible trade-off between image similarity and generation quality, with convergence guarantees under various settings.
schrodinger bridgeiterative fittingproportional fitting
ICLR 2026

Quotient-Space Diffusion Model

Problem. Diffusion models for symmetric tasks like molecular generation often use group-equivariant approaches that don't properly leverage symmetry structure.
Approach. Establish formal framework for diffusion modeling on quotient spaces, applying to SE(3) symmetry for molecular structure generation.
Contribution. Principled quotient-space approach simplifies learning and guarantees recovering target distribution, outperforming heuristic alignment strategies.
quotient space diffusionse(3) symmetrygroup action
ICLR 2026

3D Scene Prompting for Scene-Consistent Camera-Controllable Video Generation

Problem. Camera-controllable video generation struggles to maintain scene consistency when extending videos along user trajectories while handling dynamic elements across temporal boundaries.
Approach. 3DScenePrompt uses dual spatio-temporal conditioning with temporally adjacent frames for motion and spatial adjacency for consistency, employing dynamic SLAM with dynamic masking to separate static geometry from moving elements.
Contribution. Significantly outperforms existing methods in scene consistency, camera controllability, and generation quality through geometrically-consistent scene memory projections.
video generation3d reconstructioncamera control
ICLR 2026

Robust Generalized Schr\"{o}dinger Bridge via Sparse Variational Gaussian Processes

Problem. Generalized Schrödinger Bridge methods treat stage cost (task-specific obstacles and congestion penalties) as noise-free deterministic, which is unrealistic when dealing with noisy observations.
Approach. A Gaussian process prior models the pinned marginal path as a stochastic quantity, using sparse variational inference to infer the posterior path accounting for uncertainty in stage cost.
Contribution. Provides more flexible marginal path modeling that accounts for uncertainty in stage cost through principled probabilistic treatment.
gaussian processesvariational inferenceoptimal transport
ICLR 2026

Watermarking Diffusion Language Models

Problem. Diffusion language models generate tokens in arbitrary order unlike autoregressive models, making existing watermarking schemes that rely on previously generated tokens inapplicable.
Approach. The watermark applies in expectation over context even when tokens are undetermined, and promotes tokens that increase watermark strength when used as context for other tokens.
Contribution. Enables reliable DLM watermarking achieving >99% true positive rate with minimal quality impact and similar robustness to existing ARLM watermarks.
watermarkingdiffusion modelsinformation hiding
ICLR 2026

FantasyWorld: Geometry-Consistent World Modeling via Unified Video and 3D Prediction

Problem. Video foundation models lack explicit 3D grounding, limiting their spatial consistency and utility for downstream 3D reasoning tasks in embodied AI.
Approach. FantasyWorld augments frozen video foundation models with trainable geometric branch enabling joint modeling of video latents and implicit 3D field, with cross-branch supervision.
Contribution. Bridges video imagination and 3D perception, outperforming recent geometry-consistent baselines in multi-view coherence and style consistency.
video generation3d representationimplicit fields
ICLR 2026

MolEditRL: Structure-Preserving Molecular Editing via Discrete Diffusion and Reinforcement Learning

Problem. Molecular editing struggles to balance precise property optimization with structural preservation due to limitations of string-based or continuous representations in capturing discrete graph structure.
Approach. MolEditRL uses a discrete graph diffusion model pretrained on source-to-target reconstruction, followed by editing-aware RL fine-tuning that optimizes editing decisions under explicit graph constraints.
Contribution. MolEditRL achieves 74% improvement in editing success rate while using 98% fewer parameters compared to state-of-the-art methods, with comprehensive evaluation on MolEdit-Instruct (3 million examples).
discrete graph diffusionediting-aware reinforcement learningstructural constraints
ICLR 2026

Provable Separations between Memorization and Generalization in Diffusion Models

Problem. Diffusion models are vulnerable to memorization where they reproduce training data rather than generating novel outputs, but theoretical understanding of memorization mechanisms remains limited.
Approach. The paper develops a dual-separation result through statistical estimation (showing ground-truth score function doesn't minimize empirical loss) and network approximation (empirical score requires scaling network with data).
Contribution. The theoretical insights guide a pruning-based method that reduces memorization while maintaining generation quality in diffusion transformers.
statistical separationnetwork approximationpruning-based mitigation
ICLR 2026

Forward-Learned Discrete Diffusion: Learning how to noise to denoise faster

Problem. Discrete diffusion models rely on factorized reverse processes that struggle to learn target distributions in few steps, necessitating long, computationally expensive sampling procedures.
Approach. The paper introduces learnable noising (forward) process for discrete diffusion using non-Markovian formulation with learnable marginal and posterior distributions, trained end-to-end under the standard variational objective.
Contribution. Learnable forward process enables few-step generation while maintaining factorized generative process and matching the target distribution.
learnable forward processnon-markovian diffusionfew-step generation
ICLR 2026

Flow Matching with Semidiscrete Couplings

Problem. Optimal transport-based flow matching (OT-FM) promises better training but requires expensive O(n²/ε²) operations per batch pair when using Sinkhorn algorithm, limiting practical adoption.
Approach. The paper proposes semidiscrete optimal transport formulation that leverages finite-size target dataset by solving via SGD on dual potential vector of size N, matching freshly sampled noise at train time.
Contribution. Semidiscrete formulation enables practical OT-FM scaling without batch-size limitations while maintaining theoretical promises.
semidiscrete optimal transportdual potential vectorssgd optimization
ICLR 2026

Real-Time Motion-Controllable Autoregressive Video Diffusion

Problem. Real-time motion-controllable video generation faces latency constraints from bidirectional diffusion models and lacks effective autoregressive approaches that support diverse motion control without quality degradation.
Approach. AR-Drag combines autoregressive video diffusion with reinforcement learning and a trajectory-based reward model, using a Self-Rollout mechanism to preserve the Markov property while accelerating training.
Contribution. AR-Drag achieves high visual fidelity and precise motion alignment with 1.3B parameters, reducing latency significantly compared to state-of-the-art motion-controllable video diffusion models.
autoregressive diffusionreinforcement learningmotion controltrajectory reward
ICLR 2026

Pusa V1.0: Unlocking Temporal Control in Pretrained Video Diffusion Models via Vectorized Timestep Adaptation

Problem. Conventional video diffusion models use scalar timestep variables that rigidly synchronize frame evolution, limiting fine-grained temporal control and requiring task-specific retraining or narrow applicability.
Approach. Pusa V1.0 uses vectorized timestep adaptation (VTA) to enable non-destructive temporal control within a unified framework, achieving zero-shot image-to-video, video extension, and start-end frame tasks without task-specific training.
Contribution. Pusa achieves comparable image-to-video results to fine-tuned alternatives in a zero-shot manner while unlocking multiple zero-shot capabilities and preserving base text-to-video capabilities.
vectorized timesteptemporal controlvideo diffusion
ICLR 2026

LayerSync: Self-aligning Intermediate Layers

Problem. Diffusion model representation quality varies across layers but lacks mechanisms to leverage rich intermediate representations for guidance, missing opportunities for training efficiency improvements.
Approach. LayerSync regularizes diffusion models using their own intermediate representations as intrinsic guidance, where the most semantically rich representations guide weaker ones without external supervision.
Contribution. LayerSync speeds up flow-based transformer training by 8.75× on ImageNet and improves generation quality by 23.6%, generalizing across visual, audio, video, and motion domains with no pretrained models required.
self-regularizationintermediate representationtraining efficiency
ICLR 2026

WavefrontDiffusion: Dynamic Decoding Schedule for Improved Reasoning

Problem. Standard and block-based denoising strategies for diffusion language models either finalize incomplete context prematurely or break apart coherent semantic units.
Approach. Proposes WavefrontDiffusion, a dynamic decoding strategy that expands a wavefront of active tokens outward from finalized positions, following natural semantic flow.
Contribution. Achieves state-of-the-art performance on four reasoning and code generation benchmarks while producing outputs with higher semantic fidelity.
diffusion language modelsdynamic decodingwavefront scheduling

GGBall: Graph Generative Model on Poincaré Ball

Problem. Euclidean geometry fails to capture exponential complexity in hierarchical graph structures.
Approach. Introduces GGBall, combining Hyperbolic Vector-Quantized Autoencoder with Riemannian flow matching for graph generation on the Poincaré ball.
Contribution. Reduces average generation error by up to 18% on hierarchical graph datasets compared to strongest baselines.

ICLR 2026

Generalization of Diffusion Models Arises with a Balanced Representation Space

Problem. Diffusion models may memorize training samples when overfitting, limiting their generalization capability.
Approach. Analyzes memorization through representation learning lens, showing memorization yields spiky representations while generalization produces balanced ones.
Contribution. Proposes representation-based memorization detection and representation-steering method enabling controllable editing of generalized samples.
diffusion modelsrepresentation learningmemorization detection
ICLR 2026

Bures-Wasserstein Flow Matching for Graph Generation

Problem. Current graph generation approaches use linear interpolations breaking interconnected patterns, creating irregular probability paths.
Approach. Proposes BWFlow using optimal transport displacement between MRF objects to design smooth joint probability paths for co-evolving graph components.
Contribution. Achieves competitive performance with better training convergence and efficient sampling on plain and molecule generation tasks.
flow matchinggraph generationoptimal transport
ICLR 2026

Topological Flow Matching

Problem. Standard flow matching treats structured signals as Euclidean points, overlooking topological features of their domains.
Approach. Introduces topological flow matching, augmenting reference process with Laplacian-derived drift to capture underlying domain structure.
Contribution. Serves as plug-and-play replacement for standard flow matching, effective on fMRIs, ocean currents, seismic events, and traffic flows.
flow matchingtopologylaplacian eigenmaps

Efficient Zero-shot Inpainting with Decoupled Diffusion Guidance

Problem. Zero-shot inpainting methods require vector-Jacobian products through denoiser at every step, introducing significant overhead.
Approach. Proposes efficient likelihood surrogate yielding simple Gaussian posterior transitions without backpropagation through denoiser.
Contribution. Achieves strong observation consistency and coherent reconstructions while significantly reducing inference cost.
diffusion modelsimage inpaintinglikelihood-based guidance
ICLR 2026

Flow2GAN: Hybrid Flow Matching and GAN with Multi-Resolution Network for One-/Two-step High-Fidelity Audio Generation

Problem. Audio generation methods face trade-offs: GANs suffer from slow convergence and mode collapse, while diffusion methods require costly multi-step inference.
Approach. Flow2GAN combines Flow Matching training with GAN fine-tuning for few-step inference, using endpoint estimation and spectral energy-based loss for audio, plus multi-resolution network architecture.
Contribution. Delivers high-fidelity audio generation with better quality-efficiency trade-offs than existing GAN-based and Flow Matching methods, enabling one-step generation.
flow matchinggenerative adversarial networksaudio generation
ICLR 2026

CASteer: Cross-Attention Steering for Controllable Concept Erasure

Problem. Controlling diffusion model outputs for content moderation and customization requires task-specific training and struggles to generalize across concrete and abstract concepts.
Approach. CASteer applies precomputed steering vectors to cross-attention activations during inference to dynamically add or remove concepts, requiring no model retraining.
Contribution. CASteer enables precise, context-aware control over diverse tasks including harmful content removal and object replacement while preserving unaffected regions.
cross-attention steeringsteering vectorstraining-free control
ICLR 2026

RAG4DMC: Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Data-Level Modality Completion

Problem. Multi-modal datasets frequently have missing modalities, and existing generative models don't handle domain-specific completion without fine-tuning.
Approach. RAG4DMC combines in-dataset complete samples and external datasets in a dual knowledge base with feature alignment and clustering-based filtering, using multi-modal fusion retrieval to guide generation.
Contribution. RAG4DMC produces more accurate and semantically coherent missing-modality completions, improving downstream image-text retrieval and image captioning tasks.

ICLR 2026

Using maximal information auxiliary variables to improve synthetic data generation based on TabPFN foundation models

Problem. TabPFN foundation models struggle to generate realistic synthetic data when variables are weakly associated, as context examples provide limited predictive signal.
Approach. The maximal information auxiliary variable (MIAV) strategy constructs auxiliary variables by rank-matching random noise to real data, increasing context information for weak associations.
Contribution. MIAV improves synthetic data generation quality for weakly associated variables, improves computational efficiency, and shows invariance to variable order.
auxiliary variablesfoundation modelssynthetic data
ICLR 2026

Evaluating Intuitive Physics Understanding in Video Diffusion Models via Likelihood Preference

Problem. Evaluating intuitive physics in video diffusion models is challenging because physics correctness is hard to disentangle from visual appearance.
Approach. LikePhys uses the denoising objective as an ELBO-based likelihood surrogate to distinguish physically valid and impossible videos on a curated benchmark.
Contribution. LikePhys demonstrates strong alignment with human preference for physics plausibility, outperforming state-of-the-art evaluators and revealing domain-specific capacity variations across physics domains.

ICLR 2026

JointDiff: Bridging Continuous and Discrete in Multi-Agent Trajectory Generation

Problem. Generative models treat continuous data and discrete events separately, missing the synchronous interactions critical for modeling complex systems like sports dynamics.
Approach. JointDiff unifies continuous and discrete generation via a diffusion framework that simultaneously models multi-agent trajectories and possession events, with controllable guidance mechanisms.
Contribution. Achieves state-of-the-art performance on sports trajectory generation with novel weak-possessor-guidance and text-guided controllability on unified soccer/football benchmark.
diffusion modelsjoint generationconditional guidance
ICLR 2026

Delay Flow Matching

Problem. Standard flow matching with ODEs cannot model trajectory intersections, capture delay dynamics, or handle transfer between heterogeneous distributions, limiting real-world applicability.
Approach. Delay Flow Matching replaces ODE framework with Delay Differential Equations to incorporate delay terms and design initial functions for flexible distribution transfer.
Contribution. Achieves universal approximation for continuous transfer maps and enables trajectory intersections while preserving coupling relationships between distributions.
flow matchingdelay differential equationsdistribution transfer
ICLR 2026

Directional Textual Inversion for Personalized Text-to-Image Generation

Problem. Textual Inversion fails on complex prompts because learned tokens drift to out-of-distribution magnitudes that degrade conditioning in pre-norm Transformers.
Approach. Directional Textual Inversion (DTI) fixes embedding magnitude to in-distribution scale and optimizes only direction via Riemannian SGD with von Mises-Fisher prior.
Contribution. Improves text fidelity over standard textual inversion while enabling smooth semantic interpolation between learned concepts via hyperspherical parameterization.
textual inversionriemannian optimizationvon mises-fisher prior
ICLR 2026

Mod-Adapter: Tuning-Free and Versatile Multi-concept Personalization via Modulation Adapter

Problem. Multi-concept personalization for text-to-image generation requires test-time fine-tuning and struggles to customize abstract concepts like pose and lighting.
Approach. Mod-Adapter predicts concept-specific modulation directions for Diffusion Transformers using vision-language cross-attention and Mixture-of-Experts, with VLM-guided pre-training.

Problem. Fine-tuning diffusion models via policy gradient methods requires intractable marginal likelihoods; existing methods use alternative proposals and relaxations.

Contribution. Improves Stable Diffusion 2 on T2I with 8.81% relative gain over base; shows inverse noise correction improves FID on unconditional generation with lower FLOPs.
policy gradientreward shapingflow matching

Problem. Existing text-to-video generation methods struggle to maintain consistent 3D camera movements and object appearance when synthesizing views, either producing artifacts or requiring explicit 3D supervision that doesn't generalize.
Approach. Contribution. MoCa achieves accurate camera control in video generation while maintaining view, appearance, and motion consistency across frames without explicit 3D supervision.

Approach. Problem. Diffusion language model post-training methods remain largely unexplored, and their preference-inference trajectories are not aligned with post-training objectives.
Approach. TraceRL incorporates inference trajectory information into reinforcement learning for both full-attention and block-attention diffusion models, with a diffusion-based value model that accommodates process rewards.

DiVeQ: Differentiable Vector Quantization Using the Reparameterization Trick

Problem. Vector quantization's hard assignments block gradients and prevent end-to-end training, requiring auxiliary losses or temperature schedules.
Approach. DiVeQ treats quantization as adding an error vector mimicking quantization distortion while keeping the forward pass hard, enabling gradient flow without auxiliary losses; SF-DiVeQ uses space-filling curves connecting codewords for better coverage.
Contribution. DiVeQ improves reconstruction and sample quality over alternative quantization approaches on VQ-VAE and VQGAN across datasets without requiring auxiliary losses or temperature schedules.
differentiable vector quantizationreparameterization trickspace-filling curves

Problem. Diffusion models struggle with blind nonlinear inverse problems where the measurement operator is unknown and nonlinear, limiting practical applicability.
Approach. CL-DPS trains an auxiliary encoder offline using contrastive learning over randomized measurement operators to learn a surrogate for conditional likelihood, then injects gradients as guidance during diffusion sampling without estimating the operator.
Contribution. First DM-based framework for blind nonlinear inverse problems, handling rotational and zoom deblurring effectively while remaining competitive on linear benchmarks.

Approach. Asynchronous diffusion models allocate distinct timesteps to different pixels, allowing prompt-related regions to be denoised gradually and access clearer context.
Contribution. Significantly improves text-to-image alignment across diverse prompts through dynamic pixel-wise timestep modulation.
asynchronous denoisingtimestep schedulingpixel-wise diffusion

Approach. DragFlow harnesses FLUX DiT priors via region-based supervision with affine transformations for richer feature guidance, integrating personalization adapters and MLLMs.
Contribution. Leverages FLUX's stronger priors for drag-based editing with region-based paradigm; curates Region-based Dragging benchmark with multi-level annotations.

Diffusion Alignment as Variataional Expectation-Maximization

Problem. Diffusion model alignment for downstream objectives often suffers from reward over-optimization and mode collapse when using RL or direct backpropagation.
Approach. Diffusion Alignment as Variational EM (DAV) formulates alignment as iterative E-step and M-step, where E-step generates diverse reward-aligned samples via test-time search and M-step refines the model.
Contribution. DAV optimizes reward while preserving diversity on both continuous (text-to-image) and discrete (DNA sequence design) tasks.

Approach. AlphaFlow decomposes MeanFlow into trajectory flow matching and consistency, identifies negative correlation causing optimization conflict, and introduces curriculum strategy smoothly annealing objectives.

Hierarchical Semantic-Acoustic Modeling via Semi-Discrete Residual Representations for Expressive End-to-End Speech Synthesis

Problem. Speech synthesis faces a fundamental trade-off where discrete tokens ensure stability but sacrifice expressivity, while continuous signals retain richness but suffer from error accumulation.
Approach. Introduces hierarchical semantic-acoustic modeling with semi-discrete residual representations: a text-semantic language model generates plans while a residual acoustic model recovers fine-grained details via end-to-end diffusion.
Contribution. Trained on 1 million hours of speech, achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot TTS among open-source systems without requiring external discrete speech tokenizers.
semi-discrete representationshierarchical modelingend-to-end diffusion
ICLR 2026

Discrete Diffusion Trajectory Alignment via Stepwise Decomposition

Problem. Aligning discrete diffusion models with rewards typically applies rewards to final outputs and backpropagates globally, leading to computational inefficiency and incompatibility with arbitrary rewards.
Approach. Proposes stepwise decomposition that decomposes trajectory alignment into per-step posterior matching objectives, maintaining compatibility with arbitrary rewards while enabling efficient optimization.
Contribution. Achieves 12% improvement in predicted activity on DNA sequence design and improves GSM8K from 78.6 to 81.2 on LLaDA-8B, with 12% improvement on multiple domains.
stepwise decompositionposterior matchingpreference optimization
ICLR 2026

Next Visual Granularity Generation

Problem. Traditional image generation approaches lack fine-grained control over the generation process across multiple levels of visual detail.
Approach. Introduces Next Visual Granularity (NVG) generation that decomposes images into sequences of visual granularities at same spatial resolution but varying token vocabulary, progressively refining from layout to details.
Contribution. Outperforms VAR baseline with FID scores of 2.06 vs 2.09 on ImageNet and shows clear scaling behavior.

Approach. Proposes VisualPrompter, a training-free framework with self-reflection module identifying absent concepts and target-specific optimization that refines prompts at atomic semantic level.

Pyramid Patchification Flow for Visual Generation

Problem. Diffusion Transformers use constant patch size across timesteps, limiting sampling efficiency despite high-noise timesteps requiring fewer visual details.
Approach. Introduces Pyramidal Patchification Flow (PPFlow) using larger patches at high-noise timesteps and smaller patches at low-noise timesteps, shares transformer blocks across timesteps.
Contribution. Achieves 2.02x denoising speedup on SiT-XL/2 and 1.61-1.86x on FLUX.1 text-to-image with comparable quality.

Approach. BDG proposes a multi-stage diffusion approach combining a novel Multi-Angle and multi-Scale Gray Level Co-occurrence Matrix (MAS-GLCM) for fine-grained degradation discrimination, then integrating this information into a three-stage diffusion pipeline (generation, bridging, restoration).

Group Critical-token Policy Optimization for Autoregressive Image Generation

Problem. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) applies uniform optimization across all image tokens in autoregressive generation, but different tokens contribute differently to training and remain unexplored.
Approach. GCPO identifies critical tokens from three perspectives: causal dependency of early tokens, entropy-induced spatial structure, and RLVR-focused token diversity, then applies dynamic token-wise advantage weighting based on confidence divergence.
Contribution. By leveraging only 30% of image tokens, GCPO achieves better performance than GRPO with full tokens on multiple benchmarks.

Approach. Ada-RefSR proposes Adaptive Implicit Correlation Gating (AICG) that employs learnable summary tokens to distill dominant reference patterns and capture implicit correlations with low-quality features, providing adaptive regulation of reference guidance.

Self-Speculative Decoding Accelerates Lossless Inference in Any-Order and Any-Subset Autoregressive Models

Problem. Parallel token generation in arbitrary-order models violates conditional independence assumptions needed for accurate distribution matching, while discrete diffusion models struggle with parallel generation accuracy.
Approach. Any-Subset Autoregressive Models (AS-ARMs) can generate tokens in any order and parallel, supporting parallelized joint probability density estimation; Any-Subset Speculative Decoding (ASSD) corrects parallel-generated distributions via the model's own joint probability estimation.
Contribution. ASSD provably enables correct joint distribution generation with neural network calls upper bounded by predicted token count, achieves state-of-the-art on sub-200M models for infilling, and nearly matches 50X larger models on code generation.

The Limits of Inference Scaling Through Resampling

Problem. Inference scaling through resampling might allow weaker models to match stronger ones, but fundamental limits exist when verifiers have imperfect accuracy.
Approach. Analysis shows that non-zero false positive rates in verifiers impose an upper bound to resampling accuracy regardless of compute budget; this correlates strongly with single-sample accuracy.
Contribution. Optimal sampling attempts are often fewer than 10 as false positives' negative utility outweighs benefits, demonstrating fundamental limits on inference scaling with imperfect verifiers.

MVAR: Visual Autoregressive Modeling with Scale and Spatial Markovian Conditioning

Problem. Multi-scale visual autoregressive models exhibit redundancy by conditioning each scale on all previous scales and requiring tokens to attend to all preceding tokens, increasing memory and computational cost.
Approach. MVAR introduces scale-Markov and spatial-Markov assumptions to reduce complexity: only adjacent scales condition next-scale predictions, and attention is restricted to localized neighborhoods.
Contribution. MVAR reduces attention complexity from O(N^2) to O(Nk), enables training on just eight RTX 4090 GPUs, and eliminates KV cache during inference.

Source-Guided Flow Matching

Problem. Guidance of generative flow models typically requires modifying probability flow vector field, which restricts flexibility and may introduce errors through field approximation.

TextAtlas5M: A Large-Scale Dataset for Long Text Image Generation

Problem. Text-conditioned image generation has made rapid progress, yet rendering images with long-form text remains challenging due to the limitations of existing datasets, which predominantly focus on short and simple text. We introduce TextAtlas5M, a large-scale dataset designed to evaluate long-text rendering, where “long text” encompasses not only textual length but also layout complexity and semantic richness. TextAtlas5M contains 5 million generated and collected images across diverse data types, enabling comprehensive evaluation of large-scale generative models. We further curate 4,000 human-improved test cases (TextAtlasEval) spanning four domains, forming one of the most extensive benchmarks for text rendering. Evaluations show that TextAtlas5M poses substantial challenges even for state-of-the-art proprietary models (e.g., GPT-4o), with significantly larger gaps observed for open-source models. Training on TextAtlas5M consistently improves text rendering for both diffusion-based and autoregressive models, demonstrating its effectiveness for advancing text-rich image generation..
Approach. Proposes a method to address the problem.
Contribution. Text-conditioned image generation has made rapid progress, yet rendering images with long-form text remains challenging due to the limitations of existing datasets, which predominantly focus on short and simple text. We introduce TextAtlas5M, a large-scale dataset designed to evaluate long-text rendering, where “long text” encompasses not only textual length but also layout complexity and semantic richness. TextAtlas5M contains 5 million generated and collected images across diverse data types, enabling comprehensive evaluation of large-scale generative models. We further curate 4,000 human-improved test cases (TextAtlasEval) spanning four domains, forming one of the most extensive benchmarks for text rendering. Evaluations show that TextAtlas5M poses substantial challenges even for state-of-the-art proprietary models (e.g., GPT-4o), with significantly larger gaps observed for open-source models. Training on TextAtlas5M consistently improves text rendering for both diffusion-based and autoregressive models, demonstrating its effectiveness for advancing text-rich image generation..

ICML 2026

Calibrating Generative Models to Distributional Constraints

Problem. Generative models frequently suffer miscalibration, wherein statistics of the sampling distribution—such as the fraction of generations in a given class—deviate from desired values. We frame calibration as a constrained optimization problem and seek the closest model in Kullback-Leibler divergence satisfying a calibration constraint.
Approach. To address the intractability of imposing these constraints exactly, we introduce two surrogate objectives for fine-tuning: (1) the relax loss, which replaces the constraint with a miscalibration penalty, and (2) the reward loss, which converts calibration into a reward fine-tuning problem. We demonstrate that these approaches substantially reduce calibration error across hundreds of simultaneous constraints and models with up to nine billion parameters, spanning applications in protein design, image generation, and language modeling. Code is available at https://github.com/smithhenryd/cgm..
Contribution. To address the intractability of imposing these constraints exactly, we introduce two surrogate objectives for fine-tuning: (1) the relax loss, which replaces the constraint with a miscalibration penalty, and (2) the reward loss, which converts calibration into a reward fine-tuning problem. We demonstrate that these approaches substantially reduce calibration error across hundreds of simultaneous constraints and models with up to nine billion parameters, spanning applications in protein design, image generation, and language modeling. Code is available at https://github.com/smithhenryd/cgm..

Primal-Spectral Generative Modeling: Fast Analytical Generation via Pseudoinverse Lévy Inversion

Problem. A probability distribution $\mathbb{P}$ is a measure on a $\sigma$-algebra, assigning mass to sets rather than points, which poses a challenge for the training of neural networks that often struggle to reconstruct the global topology of continuous manifolds from sparse samples.
Approach. Specifically, we introduce a network, PriSpecNet, with a single-function evaluation (1-NFE) Pseudoinverse L\'evy Inversion (PiLI) solver that regards generation as a fast analytical problem, eliminating the need for iterative numerical integration while maintaining full compatibility with the stochastic interpolants.
Contribution. For ImageNet $256 \times 256$, 1-NFE PiLI achieves a competitive FID of **1.66** using only **26** Gflops, representing a **170$\times$** reduction in total Gflops compared to the 4,436 Gflops required by the 25-NFE DPM-Solver++..

ICML 2026

EPiC: Efficient Video Camera Control Learning with Precise Anchor-Video Guidance

Problem. Recent approaches for video generation with camera control often create anchor videos (i.e., rendered videos that approximate desired camera motions) to guide diffusion models as a structured prior, by rendering from estimated point clouds following camera trajectories.
Approach. However, errors in point cloud and camera trajectory estimation often lead to inaccurate anchor videos with higher training cost and low efficiency, as the model is forced to compensate for rendering misalignments. To address these limitations, we introduce EPiC, an efficient and precise camera control learning framework that constructs well-aligned training anchor videos without the need for camera pose or point cloud estimation. Concretely, we create highly precise anchor videos by masking source videos based on first-frame visibility, which ensures strong alignment, eliminates the need for camera/point cloud estimation, and thus can be readily applied to any in-the-wild video. Furthermore, we introduce Anchor-ControlNet, a lightweight module that integrates anchor video guidance in visible regions to pretrained video diffusion models, with less than 1\% of additional parameters.
Contribution. EPiC achieves efficient training with substantially fewer parameters, training steps, and less data, and generalizes robustly to anchor videos made with point clouds at test time, enabling precise 3D-informed camera control. EPiC achieves SoTA performance on RealEstate10K and MiraData for I2V camera control task. Notably, EPiC also exhibits strong zero-shot generalization to video-to-video (V2V) scenarios..
diffusion

Approach. SlerpFlow uses a geometric view based on Manifold Hypothesis: applies Spherical Linear Interpolation to rectify flow velocity directions on the hypersphere, adhering to latent space curvature.
Contribution. Achieves high-precision inversion while maintaining computational efficiency of first-order Euler solver without requiring additional training.

Problem. Unlearning in diffusion models aims to remove undesirable data while preserving utility, but these objectives conflict and require principled formulation.

Analytic Bijections for Smooth and Interpretable Normalizing Flows

Contribution. Achieves superior transition path sampling on synthetic potentials and real molecular systems including Alanine Dipeptide and Chignolin.

Approach. LaTtE-Flow introduces a layer-wise timestep-expert architecture that distributes flow-matching across specialized groups of transformer layers, each handling a distinct subset of timesteps, combined with timestep-conditioned residual attention for efficient information reuse.
Contribution. Achieves around 6× faster inference speed compared to recent unified multimodal models while maintaining competitive image generation quality.

Optimizing Visual Generative Models via Distribution-wise Rewards

Problem. Sample-wise reward functions in visual generative model training cause reward hacking, leading to mode collapse and visual anomalies that degrade image diversity.
Approach. Uses distribution-wise rewards that account for data distribution across samples rather than individual samples, with a subset-replace strategy for efficient reward estimation and RL-based post-hoc model merging optimization.
Contribution. Improves FID-50K from 8.30 to 5.77 for SiT and 3.74 to 3.52 for EDM2 while enhancing perceptual quality and preserving sample diversity.

Mode Seeking meets Mean Seeking decouples local fidelity from long-term coherence via a Decoupled Diffusion Transformer: a global Flow Matching head supervised on long videos captures narrative structure, while a local Distribution Matching head aligns sliding windows to a frozen short-video teacher via reverse-KL divergence.
Contribution. The approach enables synthesis of minute-scale videos that learn long-range coherence from limited long-video data while inheriting local realism through sliding-window alignment.
